## Document 034: 2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更 
～銀行金利市場化の長い道のり～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹 
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 人民銀がリバースレポ翌日物の日常オペを開始。短期金利の誘導精度を高める狙い 
中国の中央銀行に当たる中国人民銀行 （PBOC）が10月30日、市場に短期流動性を供給する「売り戻
し条件付き債券購入（リバースレポ）」翌日物の公開市場操作（オペ）を11月2日より毎営業日実施す
ると発表した。これまで原則として毎営業日実施していたリバースレポ7日物から切り替える。 翌日物
のオペは固定金利、数量入札の方式で行い、その金利を政策金利と位置づける。インターバンク市場
のレポ取引では翌日物が大半を占めている ことから、政策金利を7日物から翌日物に変更することで
期間のズレを解消し、短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙いがある。新たなオペ初日の金利は1.25％
であった（前営業日のリバースレポ7日物の金利は1.40％）。 
この動きは、PBOCの潘功勝行長 （総裁） が10月19日の講演で明らかにしていた金融政策運営の改革
方針を実行に移したものである。潘氏は「銀行間債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）は事実上、中
央銀行の金融政策が調節する目標金利となっているが、それと同時に、中央銀行の公開市場操作の主
要な種類と主要な政策金利の期限はなお7日で、 両者は完全には一致しない」ことを認めた上で、「適
切なタイミングで公開市場の主要な操作種類の期限を翌日物に切り替え、公開市場の翌日物レポ金利
を主要な政策金利とすることを検討している」 と予告していた。この切り替えによって、PBOCの金融
政策運営は米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）や日本銀行のそれに近づくことになる。 
さらに、潘氏は銀行貸出金利の改革を進めていることも明らかにした。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）を
参照して貸出金利を決定するこれまでの運用に加え、DR001などの短期市場金利を直接参照して金利
を決定する方法を試験的に導入したのである。今後、財務力のある企業は商業銀行と協議の上でDR001
もしくはLPRを参照した貸出金利を選択することができ、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPR を参照
した金利を適用するとしている。経済誌『財新』によれば、2026年7月以降にDR001を参照した銀行貸
出がすでに全国で100件以上実施されているという。 
PBOCが新たな銀行貸出金利の決定メカニズムを模索する背景には、LPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、
貸出金利の実勢を十分に反映できていないことがあるとみられる。 これは、2013年7月に下限規制が撤
廃される前の貸出基準金利が基準の70％まで引き下げを許容していた歴史的経緯から、信用力の高い
顧客にLPR以下の金利で貸出を実行する慣行が残っているためでもある。潘氏は改革を進めると同時
に、すでに実務的な役割を終えている貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を正式に廃止するとした。 
2026 年 11 月 9 日

### Page 2

2

■ 貸出基準金利から最優遇貸出金利へ移行。ただ、実勢金利は「最優遇」から乖離 
PBOCは2010年代以降、 銀行金利の市場化改革を慎重に推し進めてきた（図表1） 。 改革以前はPBOC
が貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を直接コントロールし、商業銀行がその上下一定の範囲内で貸出・預
金金利を決定する制度となっていたが、PBOCは2013年7月に貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃し、同年10月
には全国銀行間コール市場センター（全国銀行間同業拆借中心。以下「コール市場センター」）を通
じて貸出基礎金利（旧LPR）の発表を開始した。旧LPRは、主要9行（のち10行）の報告銀行がコール
市場センターに貸出の最優遇金利を報告し、センター側がそのうち最高値と最低値を除いた報告金利
を報告銀行の貸出残高で加重平均して算出するものであった。 
PBOCは銀行貸出金利が市場原理に基づき決定されるメカニズムへの移行を意図していたが、PBOC
が引き続き基準金利を操作して利下げを行い、報告銀行も基準金利を強く意識して最優遇金利を報告
したため、旧LPRは貸出基準金利に張り付いて変動した。PBOCは 「銀行が貸出を実行する際、多くが
なお貸出基準金利を参照して金利を決定し、特に一部の銀行が協調行動を通じて貸出基準金利の一定
図表1 銀行金利の市場化改革 
年月 No. 改革内容 
2004/10  銀行貸出金利の上限規制と銀行預金金利の下限規制を撤廃 
2013/7  ① 銀行貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の下限は貸出基準金利の 70％） 
2013/10 ② 貸出基礎金利（旧 LPR）の発表を開始 
2015/10 ③ 銀行預金金利の上限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の上限は預金基準金利の 150％） 
2019/8 ④ 貸出市場報告金利（新 LPR）の発表を開始。中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）金利を参照金利に採用 
2024/7 [1] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを開始（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の▲0.2％と＋0.5％） 
 ⑤ リバースレポ 7 日物金利を LPR の参照金利に位置づけ 
2026/6 [2] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを変更（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の±0.25％） 
2026/11 ⑥ リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを開始。その金利を政策金利に位置づけ

（出所） 中国人民銀行、月岡（2015）（2013）より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

倍率（例えば0.9倍）を隠れた下限金利として設定しており、市場金利が実体経済に伝達することを妨
げている」と指摘している。 
そこで、PBOCは2019年8月、貸出市場報告金利 （新LPR） と呼ばれる新たなメカニズムを導入する。
中長期流動性の供給ツールである中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）の金利を参照し、銀行がこれに資金
調達コストや市場需給、リスクプレミアムを反映した最優遇金利をコール市場センターに報告する方
法である。報告銀行は中小銀行を追加して18行（現在20行）まで拡大し、算出方法も加重平均から単
純平均に変更したことで、算出結果がウェイトの高い大手行の報告金利に引っ張られる傾向を排し、
指標としての包括性も高まった。 この改革により、LPRは政策金利であるMLF1年物の金利と連動する
ようになったが、LPRが「最優遇」金利から乖離した状態は依然として解消されなかった。報告銀行
が旧LPRの時と同様にMLF金利を強く意識して金利を報告したためと考えられる。 
図表3は新LPRと実際の銀行貸出金利との関係を見たものである。LPRを下回る金利での貸出比率が
年々高まっていることが分かる。LPR1年物は2019年8月～2026年10月までの間に計▲1.25％Pt引き下げ
られているが、実勢金利は資金需要の低迷もあってそれ以上に下がっていることを示唆している。 
一方、銀行預金金利は2015年に上限規制を撤廃した後も、銀行間の過度な預金獲得競争を防ぐため
窓口指導や業界の自主規制の形をとった上限管理を維持し、2022年4月になって10年物国債利回りや
LPR1年物を参照して金利を決定するメカニズムを導入した。上限管理が廃止されたわけではないが、
預金金利は近年、市場金利の低下に伴って低下傾向にある。 
■ 市場化改革の成果を銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着させられるかが今後の焦点に 
PBOCは、2019年頃までに政策金利をリバースレポ7日物金利 （短期）とMLF（中長期）の2本立てと
する金融政策運営の枠組みを整え、新LPRの導入でその枠組みを明確化した（月岡（2024）参照）。
2024年7月には、政策金利をリバースレポ7日物に一本化し、LPRの参照金利もMLFからこれに移行さ
せた。中長期金利を市場の決定に委ねる形へと歩を進めたのである。 
PBOCは同時に、誘導目標とする短期金利を一定の範囲内で安定させるため金利コリドー （回廊） の
図表3 新LPRと銀行貸出金利 図表4 金利コリドー

仕組みも整える（図表4）。2024年7月、流動性を吸収するレポと供給するリバースレポの翌日物の臨
時オペを開始し、レポ金利をリバースレポ7日物の▲0.2％Pt、リバースレポ金利を7日物の＋0.5％Ptに
設定することでコリドーの幅を狭めた。さらに、2026年6月には臨時オペの金利をそれぞれ▲0.25％Pt、
＋0.25％Ptに調整し、誘導目標とするDR001が臨時オペ金利の上下限を継続的に逸脱した場合にオペを
実施すると明確化した。リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを実施する準備を整えた形である。 
このように、PBOCは政策金利による短期市場金利の誘導精度を高めるとともに、 市場金利の変化を
実際の銀行貸出金利へと円滑に波及させるメカニズムの構築を進めてきた。今回、政策金利をリバー
スレポ翌日物金利に変更し、LPRを介さずにDR001を直接参照する貸出金利の決定方式を導入したこ
とは、一連の市場化改革における重要な節目と位置づけることができる。DR001参照型の金利は指標
金利としてのLPRを代替するものではなく、PBOCは両者を併存させながら金利波及経路を改善するア
プローチを続けるとみられる。今後は、市場化改革の成果を商業銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着さ
せられるかが焦点となろう。

は否定～」Mizuho RT EXPRESS（7月4日）

ネス・エクスプレス（10月26日）

## Document 049: 20261109 兆豐海外產業訊息評論-生成式AI模型開發商Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻

海外產業訊息評論 
全球生成式 AI 模型開發商 Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻 
企業需求帶動營收高成長，算力成本考驗高估值

報告日期：2026.11.9 研究員：吳俞潔

10 月 26 日 Anthropic(ANT US) 向美國 SEC 提交 IPO 申請，11 月 2 日更
新第三季財報，市場預期公司將於 11 月底前上市，估值達 1.5 至 2 兆。
Anthropic 旗下 Claude 主要應用於企業 AI、 軟體開發及Agent 工作流程 ，
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求快速成長，26Q3 營收達 169.6 億(季增
47%)，2026 年前三季累計營收 332 億。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年營收有
望達 580 億 ， 若以Palantir 做為高成長的企業 AI 軟體作為基準，Anthropic
也受惠企業 AI 支出增加， 營收增速高於Palantir，但未來大量算力成本以及
現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司， 對應27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，Anthropic 
IPO 估值達 1.6 至 1.8 兆，引發讓市場對企業 AI 商業化及模型公司估值的想
像空間。 
不過，兆豐認為 Anthropic 雖然已經證明企業願意為 AI 模型付費，但目前
1.6 至 1.8 兆估值已經提前反應未來數年營收維持高速成長，以及算力成本
持續改善的預期。公司 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但因算力及基礎設施費用季增
77%，導致營益率由上季 19%降至 15%，顯示收入增加並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長。另外，公司已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾，未來仍然需要擴
大 Token 需求才能支撐相關支出，考量企業 AI 需求持續成長，但模型價格
競爭及算力成本有壓縮利潤率風險， 因此兆豐目前對Anthropic 的 IPO 估值
持中性看待。

Claude Code 帶動企業 AI 需求，Anthropic 收入主要來自 Token 用量 
Anthropic 成立於 2021 年，由前 OpenAI 研究人員 Dario Amodei 創立，主
要開發 Claude 系列 AI 模型，透過 Claude 訂閱服務、企業 API 及 Claude 
Code 產品取得收入。相比傳統 SaaS 以固定訂閱費用為主，Anthropic 80%
營收來自 Token 使用量計價 ， 訂閱收入占20%， 企業可以透過AWS、Google 
Cloud 的雲端平台採購 Claude 服務，CSP 客戶占公司收入 48%，這種模式
使得 Anthropic 能隨著企業 AI 使用量增加而擴大收入，但也導致營收受到
Token 價格及企業使用量變動影響。 
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求成長，過去 12 個月支出超過 10 萬的客
戶達 11,900 家，其中超過 1,900 家年支出超過 100 萬，250 家超過 1,000

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

海外產業訊息評論 
萬，企業 AI 支出正轉向更大規模的付費使用，也帶動 Anthropic 營收快速成
長。

估值達 1.8 兆，Token 價格決定高成長能否持續 
Anthropic 2026 年前三季營收達 332 億(年增 12 倍)，26Q3 營收 170 億(季
增 47%)，但相比 Q2 季增 144%成長大幅放緩。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年
營收達 580 億，若以 Palantir 作為高成長企業 AI 軟體的估值參考 ，合理倍數
落在 27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，對應 IPO 估值 1.6 至 1.8 兆，兆豐認為雖然
Anthropic 營收成長的確遠高於多數軟體公司，Claude Code 及企業 API 的
高需求也支持未來營收持續擴張，但但模型訓練及推論需要持續投入大量算
力 ， 獲利率及現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司，以 2026 年 580 億營收估算，
隱含 28 至 31 倍 Forward P/S，反應了市場不只預期公司維持高成長，也預
期未來利潤率能隨著規模擴大而改善。 
Anthropic 80%收入來自 Token 用量計價 ， 未來營收不只取決於企業使用量 ，
也包含模型能否維持目前的高階定價， 外資8 月調查 100 名歐美大型企業 AI
預算決策者，92%預期未來 12 個月 Token 預算增加，但若 Token 價格上漲
50%，84%企業會優化使用或轉換其他模型，若價格下降 50%，86%企業則
願意增加使用量，反應企業 AI 需求仍有成長空間，但客戶對價格相當敏感，
尤其開放權重模型及低成本模型持續改善，企業有機會將部分工作轉向更為
便宜的模型，即使整體 Token 使用量增加，但開放權重和低成本模型競爭，
Anthropic 面臨單價競爭導致營收增速放緩的風險，因此兆豐認為目前 1.6 至
1.8 兆估值已經反應 Anthropic 長期維持高成長及較高 Token 價格的預期，
但如果模型競爭加劇，導致價格下降速度超過增加的使用量，將有估值下修
風險。

接近 9,000 億的算力承諾，收入成長未必同步轉為獲利 
Anthropic 為了支撐 Claude 模型訓練及企業推論需求 ， 持續擴大與Amazon、
Google、Microsoft 及其他 AI 基礎設施供應商的合作 ， 並且透過Nvidia GPU、
Google TPU 及 AWS Trainium 分散算力來源，Anthropic 目前即時可用算力
達 2.7GW，與 OpenAI 的 3.2GW 差距縮小，近期再透過 Nscale、Lambda
及 SpaceX 取得 1.1GW 的新增算力協議。不過，算力擴張也帶來龐大的長
期採購支出 ，Anthropic 已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾 ， 其中4,475 億預
計在 2030 年底前兌現， 未來12 個月到期的承諾達 698 億，合約有最低採購
要求，即使未來需求不如預期，公司仍然需要負擔相關成本。 
從財務表現來看，Anthropic 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但算力及基礎設施費用季
增 77%，營益率也由 19%下降至 15%，顯示新增收入並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長，雖然公司提到部分成本增加來自短期高價算力採購，未來隨著長
期合約及專用資料中心陸續上線，單位算力成本有機會下降，但兆豐認為即
使採購成本改善，也不代表利潤率一定回升，主要因 Anthropic 提前承諾大
規模算力支出，但未來 Token 收入仍然取決於企業實際使用量及模型定價，

### Page 3

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 3 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
若新增算力無法帶來足夠的 Token 收入 ， 固定採購成本將壓縮獲利及現金流。 
因此兆豐認為 Anthropic 目前估值不只反應營收持續高成長，也隱含未來算
力成本下降能帶動利潤率改善，但考量 26Q3 營收增速大幅放緩，營益率下
滑、未來長期算力採購承諾龐大，以及 Token 需求成長無法抵銷模型降價及
新增算力成本 ， 將壓縮利潤率 ， 不足以支撐更高的高成長成熟企業 AI 軟體公
司估值，因此兆豐對 1.6 至 1.8 兆的 IPO 估值持中性看待

（本資料僅供內部參考，不作為投資決策依據）

Update
King Yuan Electronics Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
2027 outlook remains
promising; OW
4Q26 guidance cut from the major product delay and weaker smartphone
demand: The company cut 4Q26 revenue guidance  from up >10% to high-single
digit QoQ growth, given the R-series GPU shipment delays and weaker smartphone/
consumer demand. The gross margin should also decline slightly to 36-37%, given
the capacity build but lower-than-expected utilization rate.

1Q27 and 2027 demand remains robust: However, the company reiterated that
delayed demand will be pushed out to 1Q27, suggesting 1Q27 revenue will be up at
least 10-15% QoQ and gross margin will recover to >40%. Looking ahead into 2027,
demand remains robust, and the company expects 2027 revenue to grow 40-50%
YoY with margins >40%.
KYEC in-house burn-in oven could start to support MediaTek TPU: Despite  the
shipment delay from Micro Control Company (MCC), MediaTek management still

raised its 4Q26 TPU revenue projection for both 4Q26 and 2027. We believe it
relates to KYEC's supports of its in-house burn-in oven by end-2026, which could
potentially leave more share to KYEC against ASE if MCC's oven shipments continue
to fall short in  2027.

Key Takeaways
3Q26 results largely in line with both MSe and consensus, but 4Q26 both
revenue and margin guidance missed.

Despite the near-term headwind from the supply chain leading to KYEC's weaker
performance, we expect GPU, TPU, and CPU demand to all ramp up rapidly from
1Q27.

contribution in 2028.

expansion with strong customers' commitment.

### Page 3

Vanguard International Semi. (5347.TWO)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Pricing outlook in the upward trend
Management remains constructive on the pricing environment as mature-node
supply continues to tighten. VIS emphasized its preference for negotiated price
adjustments rather than aggressively exploiting supply shortages, aiming to
maintain sustainable customer relationships. Importantly, more LTAs will expire in
2027 than in 2026, creating additional opportunities for contract repricing. In our

view, the supply-demand environment remains favorable for further pricing
improvements, although the magnitude and timing remain uncertain.
AI Revenue Contribution to Exceed 10% in 2026, With
Further Expansion in 2027
AI-related demand is becoming an increasingly important growth driver for VIS,
particularly across PMICs and discrete power devices used in data centers.
Management estimated that AI-related applications contributed a high-single-
digit percentage of revenue in 2025, rising to slightly above 10% in 2026, with

further increases expected in 2027. The company anticipates additional market
share gains in both PMICs and discrete devices as integrated device manufacturers
increasingly outsource production to foundries. The Singapore 12-inch fab will
further expand Vanguard’s exposure to AI-related technologies, including
interposers and advanced power management applications. We believe its AI
related revenue could reach 20-30% in 2027 and that should support a more

favorable product mix and reduce dependence on consumer electronics.
Given more favorable product mix and ASP trend, as well as interposer revenue
contribution, we lift our 2027 /2028 EPS by 7%/5%. We maintain our Buy rating
with lower TP at NT$160, based on lower PER of 20x of our 2027 EPS estimate
(lowered from 25x), reflecting potential policy and macro uncertainties.

Vanguard International Semi. (5347.TWO)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Vanguard International Semi.
Company description
Vanguard International Semiconductor Corporation (VIS) is a specialty IC
foundry service provider, with existing capacity mainly focused on 8-inch. VIS
offers a wide range of process technologies, including high voltage, ultra-high
voltage, and BCD. In 2024, VIS and NXP Semiconductors established the
VSMC joint venture in Singapore, with its first 12-inch fab set to begin initial

production in 2027.
Investment strategy
We rate Vanguard a Buy as we expect it to benefit from robust PMIC sales
momentum, driven by strong AI demand and improving industrial demand.
VIS is gaining market share in PMIC as clients are looking for non-Chinese
suppliers. Moreover,  we believe VIS should benefit from transfer orders from
TSMC on the 8-inch wafer process. We think VIS has already passed its
trough and expect a gradual recovery into 2027.

Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$160 is based on 20x 2027E P/E, which we see
as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth momentum and increasing
outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash
dividend policy. Our target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.8x/3.7x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along
with higher costs due to inflation or more severe ASP competition from

participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 173: 261110_daiwa_avc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_avc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: AVC announced its 3Q26 results and hosted an investor 
conference on 10 November 2026 after market hours. We believe AVC will 
be one of the key beneficiaries from the rising penetration rate of liquid 
cooling in both ASIC and GPU projects, with an aggressive capacity 
expansion plan over 2026-28. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest 
Flash, 3Q26 results preview: likely to beat expectations, on 2 November).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat the Bloomberg consensus. 
AVC’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD59,089m (+20.3% QoQ; +51.8% YoY) was 
roughly in line with our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 3Q26 
gross margin came in at 33.9% (vs. 32.6% in 2Q26 and 26.1% in 3Q25), a 
record-high level, higher than our estimate (33.5%) and the consensus 
(33.3%) on better product mix and high server revenue contribution of 66% 
in 3Q26 vs. 66% in 2Q26 and 54% in 3Q25. Its operating profit of 
TWD16,856m (+25.1% QoQ; +108.5% YoY) was in line with our estimate 
and beat the consensus by 4.2%. Thus, its operating margin of 28.5% (vs. 
27.4% in 2Q26 and 20.8% in 3Q25), a record-high level, was also in line 
with our estimate and beat the consensus (27.9%). With an FX loss of 
TWD304m, its net profit of TWD11,564m (+20.9% QoQ; +116.6% YoY) was 
in line with our estimate and beat the market consensus estimate by 3%. Its 
3Q26 EPS finished at TWD29.32.

Key highlights from the investor conference – liquid cooling revenue 
from AI servers continue to rise. AVC is confident it can maintain its 
leading position as a thermal and mechanical solution integrator, being a 
primary design partner for ASIC and GPU projects. In 2027, AVC expects 
its revenue from ASICs projects to be higher than GPU ones. From 3Q26, 
AVC has started shipping the double-sided cold plates, which it expects to 
be adopted by all ASIC clients and some GPU clients. With years of 
experience in providing CDU parts, AVC has also started shipping complete 
CDU solutions from 3Q26. AVC aims for TWD15-20bn capex in 2027 
(mainly in Vietnam, for liquid cooling capacity expansion), up from 
TWD15bn in 2026E. We keep our 2026-28E EPS broadly unchanged.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of 
TWD4,815, still based on a target PER of 30x vs. its past-3-year PER 
range of 5-25x [average PER of 15x]) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS. 
Downside risks: slower-than-expected progress in high-gross-margin 
orders, slow AI server adoption, and strong competition.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above consensus, likely on our 
more aggressive gross- and operating margins.

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

### Page 5

5

Asia Vital Components (3017 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 6 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of AVC are the same individual, but we see no issue with its board
quality. Besides, we see the Chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry, which is a
positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 2 AVC's dividend payout ratio was 40-52% over 2020-25, likely due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was 1-3% of total revenue during 2021-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chain management 1
AVC has introduced Hazardous Substance Process Management (HSPM) and received
QC080000 certification. In addition, AVC set up a Green Product team to promote HSPM and
formulate environmental regulation to make sure its material sourcing and efficiency are well-
performed.
E Data security 1
To prevent the interruption of information systems and leakage of sensitive information causing
losses to the company and customers, AVC has introduced an information security management
system and passed the ISO 27001 information security management system certification in
2019.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 297: Anthropic路演瞄准35%长期经营利润目标，算力效率提高逾4倍，大企业客户1.19万

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic路演瞄准35%长期经营利润目标，算力效率提高逾4倍，大企业客户1.19万.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 路演视频披露，公司相信⻓期能实现 35% 或更⾼的调整后经营利润率，远超今年
Q3 的 15% ；算⼒⽀出占⽐⽬标会降⾄ 45%-50% ，销售及⼈员费⽤同步压缩。截⾄ Q3 末，
Anthropic 过去 12 个⽉营收超 10 万美元的企业客⼾逾 1.19 万家，同⽐增超 12 倍；企业客⼾
净收⼊留存率 900% 。 Anthropic 展⽰礼来、乐天、 Salesforce 、沃尔玛等⼤企业客⼾应⽤
案例，显⽰ Claude 加速渗透涵盖法律、制药、软件、零售等⾏业。
摘要：
在美东时间 10 ⽇周⼆公布的 IPO 路演视频中， Anthropic 披露⻓期盈利⽬标：公司相信，随着算
⼒效率提升、直接采购和租赁算⼒的⽐例增加，以及推理业务占⽐扩⼤，⻓期调整后经营利润
率有望达到 35% 以上，较今年第三季度的 15% 提⾼ 20 个百分点以上。
除了这⼀⻓期盈利⽬标， Anthropic 此次路演还通过招股书公布了多项此前未集中披露的经营数
据：截⾄第三季度，公司营收达到 170 亿美元，同⽐增⻓超过 12 倍；过去 12 个⽉营收达到 10 万

美元以上的企业客⼾超过 1.19 万家，同样同⽐增⻓超过 12 倍；算⼒效率较 2025 年第⼀季度提⾼
超过 4 倍。 Anthropic 还披露，截⾄今年 10 ⽉末，年化营收达到约 810 亿美元。
路演材料同时展⽰了礼来、乐天、 Salesforce 、沃尔玛等⼤企业客⼾的应⽤案例，试图证明
Claude 正在从编程⼯具扩展⾄科研、法律服务、企业运营和零售等场景。
这意味着， Anthropic 正试图向投资者展⽰⼀条从⾼速增⻓⾛向⾼利润率的路径。不过，对于
35% 以上的⻓期⽬标，公司并未给出明确实现时间表；与此同时，约 9000 亿美元的算⼒承诺也
意味着，未来能否兑现盈利⽬标，仍取决于企业客⼾增⻓、算⼒成本控制以及 AI 商业化进程。
企业客⼾收⼊快速扩张，净收⼊留存率达到 900%
Anthropic 在 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇公布的 IPO 官⽅⽹站及路演材料中，⾸次集中展⽰了公司截⾄第三季度的
多项经营指标。
根据官⽅披露，截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 过去 12 个⽉营收达到 10 万美元以上的企业客⼾

超过 1.19 万家，较上年同期增⻓超过 12 倍。其中，过去 12 个⽉营收达到 100 万美元以上的客⼾超
过 1900 家，达到 1000 万美元以上的客⼾约 250 家。
客⼾留存和扩张数据同样突出。 Anthropic 披露，企业客⼾净收⼊留存率（ Net Revenue
Retention ）约为 900% ，意味着现有企业客⼾随着使⽤ Claude 的场景和规模扩⼤，带来的收⼊
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

显著增加。此外，超过⼀半的企业客⼾同时使⽤ Claude Platform 以及⾄少⼀款 Anthropic ⾃有应
⽤，⾼于 2025 年第四季度约 30% 的⽐例。
在算⼒效率⽅⾯， Anthropic 称，截⾄第三季度，公司的算⼒效率较 2025 年第⼀季度提⾼超过 4
倍。该指标按照营收与计算及基础设施⽀出的⽐值计算，即⽐较 2026 年第三季度与 2025 年第⼀
季度的营收和算⼒⽀出⽐例。
官⽅材料显⽰，这⼀指标从 2025 年第⼀季度的 0.40 倍升⾄ 2026 年第⼆季度的 2.08 倍，第三季度
回落⾄ 1.72 倍。公司解释称，第三季度效率改善受到短期算⼒供应增加的部分抵消，其中包括
50 亿美元短期算⼒采购，这部分算⼒成本明显⾼于多年期采购协议。
Anthropic 还披露，截⾄ 2026 年第三季度，过去 12 个⽉⼈均营收超过 660 万美元。公司将⼈才密
度、精简的销售体系，以及员⼯⼴泛使⽤ Claude 提升内部⼯作效率，列为改善运营效率的重要
因素。
此外，截⾄ 2026 年 10 ⽉， Anthropic 的营收运⾏率已达到约 810 亿美元。需要注意的是，运⾏率

是根据近期收⼊⽔平推算的年化指标，并不等同于已经实现的全年营收。
五家企业客⼾展⽰商业化成果， Claude 加速渗透科研和零售等领域
除了经营数据， Anthropic 还在 IPO 官⽹集中展⽰了五家企业客⼾的应⽤案例，涵盖法律服务、
制药、互联⽹、软件及零售⾏业。
其中，法律科技公司 Legora 为超过 1500 家律师事务所及企业法务团队提供服务，所有员⼯均可
使⽤ Claude Enterprise 。⼀项涉及 31 家律所的独⽴研究显⽰， Claude 每周帮助每名律师节省并
重新投⼊约 4.3 ⼩时的⾮计费⼯作时间。
制药企业礼来（ Eli Lilly ）已向超过 5 万名员⼯开放 Claude Enterprise ，关键职能部⻔中超过 85%
的员⼯积极使⽤该⼯具。 Anthropic 借此强调， Claude 的应⽤正在从软件开发扩展⾄⽣命科学和
科研领域。
⽇本乐天集团则展⽰了 Claude Code 在软件开发中的效率。该公司披露，其超过 70 个业务部⻔
正在使⽤ AI ，复杂代码修改的准确率达到 99.9% ；借助 AI ，新功能从开发到上市的⼯作⽇从 24 天

缩短⾄ 5 天。
Salesforce 称，过去⼀年公司开发⼈员的⼈均有效产出提⾼ 151% ，⼀项原本预计需要 231 个⼯
作⽇的 API 迁移任务，借助 Claude 相关⼯具仅⽤ 13 天完成。沃尔玛则披露，其 AI 购物助⼿
Sparky 的周活跃⽤⼾环⽐增⻓超过 110% ，通过该助⼿完成的订单平均客单价较⾮ Sparky 订单⾼
约 35% 。

### Page 3

这些案例显⽰， Anthropic 试图向投资者证明， Claude 的商业价值已不局限于软件开发，还在向
科研、法律服务、企业运营和消费者零售等场景扩展。与此同时，公司披露，全球 2000 强企业
中，超过 30% 的企业过去 12 个⽉在 Anthropic 的消费达到 10 万美元以上；财富 500 强企业中这⼀
⽐例超过 50% 。但平均⽽⾔，财富 500 强客⼾在 Claude 上的⽀出仍不⾜其总费⽤的 0.1% ，公司
认为这意味着企业市场仍有较⼤扩张空间。
算⼒⽀出占⽐⽬标降⾄ 45% ⾄ 50% ，销售及⼈员费⽤同步压缩
据路演视频， Anthropic ⾸席财务官 Krishna Rao 表⽰，公司相信⻓期能够实现 35% 或更⾼的调整
后经营利润率，⽽ 2026 年第三季度这⼀指标为 15% 。
为实现这⼀⽬标， Anthropic 预计，随着持续提升算⼒效率、增加 Anthropic Direct 算⼒供应⽐
例，以及更多算⼒资源转向推理业务，计算与基础设施⽀出占营收的⽐例将逐步降⾄约 45% ⾄

50% 。这⼀⽐例在第三季度为 58% 。
Anthropic Direct 是公司直接租赁 AI 芯⽚和数据中⼼、⽽⾮完全依赖云服务商提供算⼒的模式。
通过直接获取和管理算⼒资源，公司希望提⾼供应链控制⼒，并降低⻓期算⼒成本。
其他费⽤也被纳⼊⻓期优化⽬标。路演视频显⽰，销售、营销及合作伙伴费⽤占营收的⽐例预
计将降⾄ 7% ⾄ 10% ，第三季度为 14% ；经调整的⼈员及其他运营费⽤占⽐则预计降⾄ 7% ⾄
10% ，第三季度约为 13% 。
从这⼀⻓期成本结构看， Anthropic 希望将计算与基础设施⽀出控制在营收的⼀半左右，同时进
⼀步压缩销售、营销、⼈员及其他运营费⽤的相对占⽐，以推动整体调整后经营利润率迈向
35% 以上。
值得注意的是， Anthropic 在官⽅材料中还披露， 2026 年 9 ⽉公司代码库中超过 90% 的代码贡献
由 Claude 完成。这⼀数据被公司⽤于说明 AI 不仅是其对外销售的产品，也已深度融⼊⾃⾝研发
和运营流程。
调整后盈利表现仍有波动， GAAP ⼝径第三季度继续亏损

Anthropic 招股书披露， 2026 年第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，较上年同期的 13.82 亿美元⼤
幅增⻓；调整后经营利润为 24.63 亿美元，对应调整后经营利润率 15% ，低于第⼆季度的 19% 。
但在 GAAP 会计⼝径下， Anthropic 第三季度仍录得 5.67 亿美元经营亏损，与第⼆季度 9.74 亿美
元经营利润形成反差。公司解释称，第三季度经营业绩受到约 50 亿美元短期算⼒采购成本，以
及约 20 亿美元⾮现⾦捐赠匹配计划费⽤的影响。
此外，第三季度 GAAP 净亏损达到 233 亿美元，其中包括约 219 亿美元因可转换票据、衍⽣负债
及 SAFE 协议公允价值变化产⽣的⾮现⾦费⽤。这⼀项⽬与公司⽇常经营亏损并⾮同⼀概念。
调整后经营利润率与 GAAP 经营利润率也并⾮同⼀指标。 Anthropic 将调整后经营利润定义为剔
除股权激励费⽤、⾮现⾦慈善捐赠及 Bartz 诉讼和解费⽤后的经营利润；调整后经营利润率则为
调整后经营利润除以营收。投资者需要注意，这⼀⾮ GAAP 指标排除了部分实际计⼊财报的费

⽤，不能直接等同于公司按照 GAAP 标准实现的盈利⽔平。
从更⻓时间看， 2026 年前九个⽉ Anthropic 营收达到 332.42 亿美元， GAAP 经营亏损为 12.6 亿美
元，但调整后经营利润达到 40.81 亿美元，对应调整后经营利润率约 12% 。同期，公司上年同期
调整后经营亏损为 31.33 亿美元。

### Page 4

这表明 Anthropic 的调整后经营表现已经明显改善，但季度之间仍有波动。公司在招股书中也提
醒，新增算⼒投⼊通常先于相关收⼊兑现，未来随着⼤规模算⼒陆续上线，经营利润率和调整
后经营利润率仍可能阶段性承压。
约 9000 亿美元算⼒承诺，未来数年仍需⼤规模投⼊
在展⽰算⼒效率提升和利润率改善前景的同时， Anthropic 也披露了庞⼤的⻓期算⼒投⼊计划。
根据 IPO 官⽅事实说明书，截⾄ 2026 年第三季度， Anthropic 的算⼒承诺总额约为 9000 亿美元。
其中，约 4516 亿美元预计将在 2030 年底前发⽣，其余⼤部分预计在 2031 年⾄ 2045 年间发⽣。
未来 12 个⽉内到期的相关承诺约为 707 亿美元。
作为资⾦储备，截⾄第三季度末，公司持有约 960 亿美元现⾦及现⾦等价物，以及约 360 亿美元
短期投资。公司还披露，累计融资约 1410 亿美元。
这意味着，尽管 Anthropic 希望通过提⾼算⼒效率和优化成本结构，将⻓期调整后经营利润率推

升⾄ 35% 以上，公司仍需要持续投⼊巨额资⾦建设算⼒基础设施。如何平衡前沿模型研发、算
⼒采购和盈利能⼒，将成为上市后投资者持续关注的问题。
⾼盛分析师预计， Anthropic 到 2030 年的年度营收有望达到约 6850 亿美元，其中企业客⼾贡献约
5860 亿美元。不过，这属于分析师预测，并⾮公司给出的业绩承诺。
创始团队仍将保留⼤额持股，出售股份约占 IPO 发⾏规模 2.9%
在盈利⽬标之外， Anthropic 创始团队的股票出售安排也成为此次 IPO 招股书的⼀⼤看点。
根据 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇更新的招股书， Anthropic 七位联合创始⼈均计划出售 337.84 万股股票，按每股 74
美元的发⾏价区间中值计算，每⼈套现⾦额约 2.5 亿美元，七⼈合计约 17.5 亿美元。
七位创始⼈包括⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei 、总裁兼董事会主席 Daniela Amodei ，以及 Tom
Brown 、 Jack Clark 、 Jared Kaplan 、 Sam McCandlish 和 Christopher Olah 。

尽管出售规模达到⼗亿美元级别，但按发⾏价中值计算，每位创始⼈仍将保留价值约 180 亿美元
的持股。彭博亿万富翁指数估算， IPO 定价对应的七位创始⼈个⼈财富均有望达到约 180 亿美
元。
创始⼈出售股票也并⾮⾸次。招股书显⽰， Dario Amodei 和 Daniela Amodei 此前曾参与 2025 年 6
⽉及 2026 年 4 ⽉的股份要约收购交易。此次 IPO 中，七⼈合计出售的股份约占公司本次公开发⾏
股份总数的 2.9% ，⽽公司本⾝并不会获得这部分⼆级市场出售所得。
IPO 最⾼募资 644 亿美元，另有 260 亿美元私募配售
Anthropic 此次 IPO 计划发⾏ 8.25 亿股 A 类普通股，其中公司发⾏约 8.014 亿股，现有股东出售约
2365 万股，发⾏价区间为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元。按发⾏价区间上限计算， IPO 募资规模最⾼约 644 亿
美元。
此外，公司还安排了总额 260 亿美元的同步私募配售，认购⽅包括 AMD 、博通、⾕歌、微软、英

伟达、美光和台积电等科技企业。若将 IPO 与私募配售合并计算，整体融资规模最⾼可达 904 亿
美元。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

的股数计算，估值最⾼约 1.02 万亿美元；彭博社报道的约 1.4 万亿美元估值则纳⼊了更⼴泛的潜
在股权稀释因素。两者并⾮完全相同的估值⼝径。
Anthropic 官⽹还披露， IPO 路演于 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇启动，预计 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇确定最终发⾏价，股票预计于
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇在纳斯达克开始交易，股票代码为 ANT 。
对于投资者⽽⾔， Anthropic 此次路演不仅是在推介其快速增⻓的营收和模型竞争⼒，也是在解
释公司如何从⼤规模算⼒投⼊⾛向持续盈利。 35% 以上的⻓期调整后经营利润率⽬标、算⼒效
率提⾼逾 4 倍、超过 1.19 万家⼤型企业客⼾，以及约 9000 亿美元的⻓期算⼒承诺，共同构成了这

场超⼤型 IPO 的关注焦点。
但在⾼增⻓预期之外，算⼒投⼊能否转化为⾜够的收⼊和利润，以及企业客⼾能否持续扩⼤ AI 服
务⽀出，仍是市场评估 Anthropic ⻓期价值时绕不开的核⼼问题。

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 325: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

## Document 337: daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109

Source PDF: all report pdf/daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  運動用品  ‧美國

Under Armour 
(UAA.O/UAA US)

客流量下滑考驗 
重要訊息  
Under Armour  2Q FY27 (7-9 月；財年止於 3 月) 營運約當 損平。儘管 FY27
營收年減中個位數之 展望不變，管理層下調毛利率指引，反映考量油價、
運價和庫存清倉等衝擊迎面而來 。 
評論及分析  
2Q FY27 營運保持損平 。2Q FY27 每股盈餘 0.00 美元，符合預期。營收
12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品 /通路需求 皆轉差。地區別
方面，北美、歐非中東及亞太平洋銷售分別年減 7%、9%、9%，僅拉美
為唯一成長市場 ，年增 3%。再加上通 路、地區組合不利與折扣力道加大，
毛利率延續衰退走勢， 年減 0.3、季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率
至僅 1.2%，年減 0.1、季減 3.1 個百分點。  
產業困境 。管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，預計
3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9% (連三季惡化 )，劣於市場共識之 4%衰退；其中，
北美及歐非中東將為 風暴中心， 分別年減低雙位數、高個位數，亞太則 將
改善至約年持平。加上油價和運價上漲、庫存清倉與匯率不利 等因素疊加
影響，FY27 毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增
1.7-2.2 個百分點中約有 1.5 個百分點係來自美國退稅挹注 。 
庫存去化策略 。2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，
仍高於過去十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因營運規模縮減凸顯在途存貨
較高。管理層表示 正與合作工廠協商推遲部分發貨，同時考慮 2H FY27 從
批發端去化庫存，但將避開同業清倉甩賣的場所。 公司計畫持續減少產品
SKU、聚焦高端或細分領域 (例如美式足球釘鞋已成為美國第二大品牌 )，
同時簡化工作流程 ，確保長期利潤率改善空間 。 
投資建議  
考量歐美客流量逆風 與促銷影響 仍延續，我們維持 Under Armour 「持有」
評等，目標價改採 FY28 每股淨值 1.6 倍推算，調整為 5.5 美元。 
投資風險  
上檔風險為消費較預期強勁與品牌 重拾人氣。

### Page 2

美國 Under Armour

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Under Armour 11 月 6 日法說會重點摘要  
 2Q FY27 (財年止於 3 月；7-9 月) 每股盈餘為 0.00 美元，符合預期。
營收 12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品及全通路需求皆
轉差。受通路、地區組合不利與 折扣力道加大 影響，毛利率年減 0.3、
季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率至僅 1.2%。 
 產品別營收：服飾  (佔總營收 71%)、鞋類 (19%) 及配件  (8%) 全面
惡化至年減 6%、11%、7%。 
 市場別營收：北美 (佔總營收 59%)、歐非中東  (23%) 及亞太平洋  
(13%) 分別年減 7%、9%、9%，符合公司指引。拉美  (4%) 年增
3%，為唯一成長市場。 
 通路別營收：批發業務  (佔總營收 58%) 年減 6%，再度轉弱，主因
全價商品銷售疲軟及部分分銷管道遇 逆風。直接面對消費者  (DTC，
40%) 衰退擴大至 8%，反映客流量壓力導致 直營店與電商業務分別
年減 7%、12%。 
 2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，仍高於過去
十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因在途存貨較高 。公司正透過與合作
工廠協商推遲部分發貨， 同時考慮 2H FY27 從批發端去化庫存 ，但將
避開同業清倉甩賣 的場所。  
 FY27 營收維持年減中個位數之 展望，但基於油價和運價上漲、庫存
清倉與匯率不利等因素影響， 將毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次
為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增 1.7-2.2 個百分點中 約有 1.5 個百分點係 來自
美國退稅挹注。加上組織轉型與重組費用認列，預計每股虧損 0.02
至每股盈餘 0.02 美元。 
 短期而言，管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，
預計 3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9%；其中，北美及歐非中東將分別年減
低雙位數、高個位數，亞太約呈年持平。 加上毛利率年增 0.3-0.5 個
百分點展望， 3Q FY27 營運落後市場預期。

圖 6: 公司概況  圖 7: 服飾貢獻營收最大比重 
成立於 1996 年， Under Armour 為國際領導運動品牌，致力
提供專業運動服飾、鞋類及配件；其產品採用創新材料技術，
包括吸濕排汗、溫度控管、緩衝與布料永續利用等，目的在
創造差異化，並提升消費者品牌 體驗。美洲為 FY26 營收主要
來源地區，佔比達 58%，其次分別為歐非中東 24%、亞太平
洋 14%、拉丁美洲 4%。

股價說明  等級 定義 
 增加持股  (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的 總
報酬。  
 持有  (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 降低持股  (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 未評等 (NR) 凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。  
 受法規限制  
未評等 (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和 /或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交
流，其中包括提供評等給投資人參考。  
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價 -現價)/現價

## Document 355: France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 8 October 2026

EcoFlash

France: double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and 
stable non-performing loans ratio

Business creations rose by 12% y/y over the first eight months of 2026 1. This is significantly higher than the 5.3% y/y 
growth recorded in 2025. This increase was driven by sectors linked to information and communication technologies.

Meanwhile, business bankruptcies rose by 5.3% y/y between January and July, according to data from the Banque de 
France (compared with the 3.6% y/y increase for the whole of 2025). The rise was more pronounced in the 1st quarter (+8% y/y) 
before slowing down. This slowdown could persist, as, according to Altares, payment delays have fallen to an average of 12.3 
days in the second quarter of 2026, compared with 14.1 days a year earlier (and 13.1 days for the European average).

Rising bankruptcies are more heavily affecting the services sector, which is also experiencing slightly stronger growth 
in business creations. This correlation is particularly noticeable in the retail sector, as business creations have risen by 13% 
since the start of 2026, while bankruptcies are up by 3%. In construction and manufacturing, business bankruptcies have been 
falling since the second quarter of 2026.

The average ratio of non -performing corporate loans has remained stable at a moderately high level for the past year  
(4.0% of total loans and advances in Q2 2026). In construction, where activity is more cyclical than in other sectors, the ra tio of 
non-performing loans has reached its highest level since 2019, standing at 9.5%.

The proportion of loans that have deteriorated but are not yet non-performing (“Stage 2” according to IFRS 9 terminology) 
is stable, standing at 15.4% q/q, and remains below its all -time high (16.1%). This indicator, which helps to anticipate trends in 
non-performing loans, is relatively encouraging.

Despite a significant increase since 2021, the economic weight of bankruptcies remains, on average, below its peak in 
Q1 2025 (0.53 compared with 0.61, respectively). This indicator generally allows bankruptcies to be weighted based on the size 
of the businesses. However, the economic weight of bankrupted small businesses is at a historically high level (2.11).

Take a look at our EcoCharts on trends in business creations and bankruptcies in France and the 
associated risks. It will be updated on a quarterly basis.

Thomas Humblot 
Economiste, team: Banking economics 
thomas.humblot@bnpparibas.com

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced economies, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

1 For more details, see Business creations and bankruptcies in France: double-digit growth in business creations, lower growth in bankruptcies, 
and stable non-performing loans, 8 October 2026.

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG October 30, 2026
WEEKLY AGGREGATES
ITEM DEFINITIONS AND METHODOLOGY
3© 2026 LSEG.  All marks herein are used under licence. TRPR_82222_875
General:
All aggregates are bottom-up aggregates calculated from analysts' consensus forecasts unless otherwise noted. Bottom-up
aggregates are weighted calculations using the I/B/E/S data from LSEG share class monitored or the official index weight of
the index-provider. Top-down forecasts are compilations of strategists' estimates. I/B/E/S universe calculations are based on

all companies within a given market monitored by LSEG.
Benchmark index calculations, including those for official local market indices, the S&P 500 Index, and the FTSE World Equity
Indices are calculated using official index constituents and weightings. All index composites are representative of current
index constituents. Index constituents and weightings are updated monthly during options expiration (the Thursday before the
third Friday of every month) for every index except the S&P 500, the constituents of which are current as of the date of

calculation.
PEIGBY refers to the Price Earnings Inverse Government Bond Yield ratio, which measures equity valuation with respect to
the inverse-yield on long-term government bonds (now known as the 'Fed' model). All PEIGBY ratios are I/B/E/S universe-
specific except for the US ratio, where it is based on the S&P 500 Index.
Methodology:
For US and selected Canadian aggregates, calendar-year aggregation is performed using the sum of four quarters' estimates

and a quarterly pro-ration is applied to non-December year end companies' quarterly EPS estimates. Outside of the US and
Canada, all company-level data are calendarized prior to aggregation using the Compustat method of calendarization.
Aggregation is performed using the annual consensus EPS estimate and a May roll-back is applied to December year-end
markets. Aggregates for Japan, where most companies report in March, use an August rollback and a November rollback is

used for Australia and New Zealand, where the fiscal year-end for most companies is in June.
All 12-month-forward data for ex-US markets are calculated using a pro-rata weighting to annual EPS forecasts. The
weightings applied are a daily calibration out of 365 days relative to each constituent's individual fiscal year-end and the
current date.
All non-surprise data, including aggregate forecast earnings, forecast growth, and forward-looking valuation calculations are

weighted according to the I/B/E/S share class monitored for I/B/E/S universes or the weightings assigned to each constituent
in a given index. Aggregated earnings calculations are the equivalent of net profit aggregates, as they are derived from the
EPS forecasts aggregated to the net income level by the appropriate share weighting.
Earnings Aggregation:
For 12-month-forward earnings, the 12-month-forward EPS is used in place of the calendarized EPS in the formulas below.

I/B/E/S universe EPS Aggregate = Index EPS Aggregate =
Growth Calculations:
All growth calculations are the weighted year over year growth in earnings for the calendarized fiscal year period. The
formulas below use index shares for index growth calculations.
Calendarized Earnings Growth = 12-month-forward Growth =
Valuation:
All valuation calculations are the weighted-average price-earnings ratio for the fiscal period in question. The formulas below

use index shares for index P/E calculations.
Calendarized P/E Aggregate = 12-month-forward P/E =
Revisions Momentum:
Unless otherwise indicated, analysts' revisions momentum is a simple aggregation of the total number of estimates raised
divided by the total number of estimates lowered. Thus, any revisions ratio less than 1 indicates negative revisions
momentum while a ratio greater than 1 indicates positive revisions momentum, with 1 marking parity.

The Global Aggregates from LSEG product contains annual aggregates for universes included in this publication, including
supplemental aggregate data of over 100 items as well as history dating back to 1985 for North America and to 1987 for other
markets. To learn more about this product, contact your LSEG sales representative.
FTSE and the FTSE World Equity Indices are owned by FTSE International Limited.

## Document 389: Monthly household spending indicator_ Spending resilient despite headwinds (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Monthly household spending indicator_ Spending resilient despite headwinds (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
1
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
HOUSEHOLD SPENDING
INDICATOR BULLETIN
Spending resilient despite headwinds
4 November 2026
Key points
• Household spending rose 0.6%mth in
September, returning to growth after
August’s brief decline. Q3 spending increased
2.1%qtr, while volumes rose a more subdued

0.7%qtr, pointing to a sharp acceleration
in price growth through the quarter.
• Services spending rebounded and goods
spending remained supportive. Non-
discretionary spending outperformed
discretionary categories, with higher fuel
spending providing significant support.
• In real terms, spending growth over the
quarter was led by transport but with
most other categories also recording
gains. All states except Tasmania
recorded positive volume growth

in Q3, led by WA and Victoria.
• Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in
line with expectations and consistent with
our consumption outlook ahead of the Q3
National Accounts (due 2 December).
Q3 real household
spending:
0.7%qtr, 3.2%yr

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel  which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.

## Document 391: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.

New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.
9 Nov 2026 |  Westpac Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz
Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

4
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Labour, the Greens and Opportunity share support
for increased public and social investment, a broader
tax base, greater infrastructure investment, stronger
competition policy, cheaper public transport and a
more active role for government. Labour and the Greens
overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while
Labour and Opportunity share an emphasis on social

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

based policy.

and differences.

## Document 530: 不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド ～多様な運用戦略とREITとの補完関係～

Source PDF: all report pdf/不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド ～多様な運用戦略とREITとの補完関係～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/4 
不動産市場で存在感を増す私募ファンド 
～多様な運用戦略と REIT との補完関係～ 
 資産運用調査部 シニア研究員  河谷 善夫

1．大きく残高を増やしてきた私募ファンド 
資料 1 は、我が国の不動産証券化の主体別保有資産額の推移と、2015 年度から2025 年度までの
年平均増加率（CAGR）を示したものである。

資料 1 不動産証券化の主体別保有資産額の推移（左）と年平均増加率（CAGR）（右） 
<保有資産額の推移>    <年平均増加率（CAGR）>

（注 1）J-REIT、私募リート、不動産特定共同事業は各年度末、私募ファンドは各年度の 12 月末時点。年平均増加率は、それぞ
れの系列の 2015 年度と 2025 年度の比較値から算出。なお、資産額は、保有する不動産・信託受益権の残高を示し、年間の
取得額や投資家の出資額ではない。 
（注 2）不動産特定共同事業とは、不動産特定共同事業法に基づき、国土交通大臣や都道府県知事などの許可・登録を受けて
行われるもの。複数の投資家から出資金を募り、不動産を売買・賃貸・運用して得られた利益を分配する事業。不動産クラウド
ファンディングの多くが活用し、少額からの投資が可能となっている。 
（注 3）不動産私募ファンドには、広義には私募リートも含まれる。私募リートは、投信法に基づく非上場の投資法人である。運用
期間を定めず、一定の条件の下で投資口の払戻しに応じる（オープンエンド）。一方、それ以外の私募ファンドには、合同会社
や特定目的会社等を用い、運用期間を定め、投資家の請求による期中の払戻しを行わないものが多い。本稿では、私募リート
を除く不動産私募ファンドを「私募ファンド」と呼ぶ。なお、私募リート以外の私募ファンドにも、無期限・オープンエンド型の商品
がある。 
（出所）国土交通省「不動産証券化の実態調査」及びARES J-REIT Databook（2026 年）を基に第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作
成

2015 年度時点の比較では、J-REITの保有資産額が最も大きかったが、2020 年代に入り、私募フ
ァンド、私募リートの保有資産額は、J-REITを上回るペースで増加している。 
2025 年度時点でみると、私募ファンドの保有資産額は約39.5 兆円（2025 年 12 月末）と、J-

### Page 2

2/4 
REITの約24.5 兆円（2026 年3 月末）を約15 兆円上回っている。この間の年平均増加率で最も大
きいのは、私募リートだが、私募ファンドも年11％を超える伸びを示した。 
もっとも、保有資産には借入金を活用して取得したものも含まれるため、その残高増加を、投
資家からの新規出資額の増加と同一視することはできない。また、残高の増加だけで運用成績を
判断することもできない。一方、不動産の保有・運用を担う主体として、私募ファンドの存在感
が増していることは確認できる。近年の拡大ペースの違いを考えるうえでは、資金調達経路の違
いも重要である。J-REITでは、投資口価格の低迷が公募増資を通じた物件取得の制約となり得
る。実際、2025 年は、投資口価格が1 口当たりの純資産価値（NAV）を下回る銘柄が多く、公募増
資が低調だった。一方、私募ファンドは、投資家の意向に応じた投資対象・運用戦略を設定して
出資を募る仕組みであり、上場市場とは異なる経路で不動産投資資金を受け入れている。こうし
た違いが、近年の資産拡大ペースの差に関係している可能性がある。 
本稿では、私募ファンドの仕組みや運用戦略を整理し、REITとの役割の違いを踏まえて、今後
の注目点を示す。

2．私募ファンドの仕組みと資金の出し手 
私募ファンドは、特定の投資家から集めた出資金と金融機関からの借入金等を用いて、不動産
を取得・運用する仕組みである。代表的な形態には、合同会社が匿名組合出資を受けるGK-TKスキ
ーム（資料2 左）や、特定目的会社を用いるTMKスキーム（資料2 右）がある。運用会社（アセッ
トマネジメント会社）が投資対象や運用方針に応じて物件の取得・管理・売却等を行う。

資料 2 私募ファンドの仕組みの概要 
<GK-TKスキームの例>    <TMKスキームの例>

（注）匿名組合出資は、合同会社が行う事業に出資し、その利益の分配を受ける契約に基づく出資。優先出資は、TMKの特定出
資に対し、利益配当又は残余財産の分配で優先する出資であり、借入金・社債より優先するものではない。特定出資等は図示を
省略。 
（出所）第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

GK-TKスキームでは、合同会社が投資家からの匿名組合出資と借入金により、通常、不動産信託
受益権を取得する。不動産自体は信託銀行等が所有し、合同会社はその収益等を受け取る。一
方、TMKスキームでは、特定目的会社が優先出資や借入金・社債等によって資金を調達し、不動産
を直接所有するか、不動産信託受益権を保有する。

### Page 3

3/4 
私募ファンドは、こうした法的な仕組みだけでなく、運用戦略や投資目的も多様である。資料3
では、その代表例を整理する。

資料 3 私募ファンドの主な運用戦略・目的

（注）代表例を示したものであり、各区分は必ずしも相互に排他的ではない。 
（出所）第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

私募ファンドに出資する投資家としては、国内外の年金基金、保険会社、金融機関、政府系フ
ァンド、事業会社等がある。 
こうした資金を受け入れるファンドの運用戦略は、資料3 に示したように、安定した賃料収入
を重視するものから、開発等による価値向上を目指すものまで幅広い。このように私募ファンド
は、単にJ-REITより流動性が低い投資商品というだけでなく、投資対象、保有期間、開発・改修
の有無等に応じた多様な投資機会を提供するものといえる。 
流動性の低さは投資上の制約であるが、長期資金を持つ投資家にとっては、それだけで投資対
象から外れるわけではない。投資対象や運用戦略を選択し、専門的な運用を通じて収益を獲得す
る手段として、私募ファンドが活用されている。

3．REITとの役割の違いと今後の注目点 
J-REITや私募リートが主として不動産の継続的な保有・運用による賃料収入を重視するのに対
し、私募ファンドには、開発や改修による価値向上と、その後の売却を組み合わせるものもあ
る。長期保有型も含む多様な戦略の中で、開発・価値向上段階のリスクを引き受ける資金の受け
皿となっている点に、私募ファンドの役割の一つがある。 
私募ファンドのうち、あらかじめ運用期間を定める有期ファンドでは、物件売却による資金回
収を予定する場合、その時期も運用計画に組み込まれる。契約等に基づいて運用期間を延長でき
る場合もあるが、延長の可否・条件や、必要に応じた借換えへの対応を考慮する必要がある。こ
のため、運用期限を設けないJ-REITや私募リートとは異なり、想定した時期・価格で売却できる
かに加え、売却を延期する場合の条件にも注意が必要である。 
また、私募ファンドの中には、将来のREIT等への売却を想定して物件を一時的に保有する「ブ
リッジファンド」もある。REITへの売却を想定するこうしたファンドでは、売却先となるREITの
取得余力も重要となる。このため、私募ファンドの拡大を、REITに代わる投資先の成長とのみ捉
えるのは十分ではない。開発や価値向上を担い、REIT等の長期保有主体に物件を供給する補完的

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用

4Q26 產能利用率及 ASP 持續提升，展望優於預期 
3Q26產能利用率約 90%符合預期，晶圓出貨 75.1萬片，季增 5.3%、年增
11.6%，PMIC比重增加 5%至 82%，調整後稅後 EPS為 1.65元，較原預
估的 1.69元略低 2.1%。4Q26訂單能見度約 4個月，AI需求維持強勁，世
界對 4Q26展望為產能利用率 90%以上，月產能季增 0.36%至 28萬片約當
8吋晶圓，晶圓出貨量季增 0~2%，ASP季增 3~5%，毛利率 34~36%(以 1
美元兌 32 元新台幣)，折舊 23.5 億元、季增約 2%，累計 2026 年折舊約
90.7億元，低於前次說法 92.5億元。上修 4Q26營收約 2.6%至 166.3億元，
季增 5.9%、年增 32.1%，稅後 EPS為 1.89元，較前次預估上修約 4.4%。 
VSMC 廠 1Q27 量產，預期 2027 年占營收 10%、並達成損平 
2026 年資本支出維持 600~700 億元，其中 90%用於新加坡廠，新加坡
VSMC 12 吋廠維持 1Q27量產的規劃，預期 2028年底達到每月 4.4萬片產
能，並維持 90%以上產能利用率，其中 50%以上營收將來自台積電外包的
Interposer，預估 2027 年折舊顯著增加超過 40%，但整體產品組合佳，且
技術及設備來自台積電、大幅縮短學習曲線，仍預期 2027 年底即可達成損
平，預估 2027年 12吋晶圓將占營收 10%、2028年提升至 21%。 
仍看好 2027 年 12 吋廠及 AI 應用貢獻，目前 PE 評價偏低 
將 2026/2027 年營收預估分別上修 1.3%/1.3%至 591.1 億元/798.7 億元，
2027年起折舊將因 VSMC量產而大幅提高，將 2026/2027年稅後 EPS預
估調整至 6.32元/8.57元、分別上修 0.7%/下修 11.1%。短期股價修正，但
以 2027年獲利計算目前 PE評價僅約 14倍，低於過去 10年平均約 20倍，
且仍看好 2027 年起跨入 12 吋業務、AI 比重提升，將有利於評價回升，維
持買進建議，目標價 170元(20X 2027F EPS)反應獲利預估因折舊而下調。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 PMIC 占營收達 82%，4Q26 訂單能見度約 4 個月，產能利用率
提升至 90%以上，營運成果及展望皆優於預期。 
⧫  
12 吋廠 1Q27 量產，預期 2028 年底達月產能 4.4 萬片，產能利用率將
維持 90%以上，預估 2027/2028 年 12 吋晶圓將占營收 10%/21%。 
⧫  
2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分別上修 0.7%/下修 11.1%至 6.32 元/8.57
元，主要反映 2027 年折舊大幅增加，股價修正後 PE 評價僅 14 倍偏
低，維持買進建議，目標價 170 元(20X 2027F EPS)。

營運分析
3Q26 PMIC 需求強勁，營運成果符合預期
3Q26 營收 157 億元優於預期約 2.3%，產能利用率約 90%符合預期，晶圓出貨 75.1 萬片，季增
5.3%、年增 11.6%，AI Server 及手機 PMIC需求強勁，帶動 0.18μm製程比重提升，PMIC比重
則增加 5%至 82%，毛利率 33.6%略低於原預估的 34.1%，調整後稅後 EPS為 1.65元，較原預估
的 1.69元略低 2.1%。

4Q26 產能利用率及 ASP 持續提升，展望優於預期
4Q26訂單能見度約 4個月，應用面來看 AI需求維持強勁，車用穩定、工控穩健成長，但消費性需
求疲弱、特別是 PC/NB，世界對 4Q26 展望為產能利用率將再微幅提升至 90%以上，月產能季增
0.36%至 28萬片約當 8吋晶圓，晶圓出貨量季增 0~2%，ASP季增 3~5%，毛利率 34~36%(以 1
美元兌 32元新台幣)，各製程比重約與 3Q26相同，折舊 23.5億元、季增約 2%，累計 2026年折
舊約 90.7億元，低於前次說法 92.5億元。因 ASP提升幅度優於預期，上修 4Q26營收約 2.6%至
166.3億元，季增 5.9%、年增 32.1%，毛利率由 34.6%上修至 35.1%，主要來自較低的折舊，稅
後 EPS為 1.89元，較前次預估上修約 4.4%。

VSMC 廠 1Q27 量產，預期 2027 年占營收 10%、並達成損平 
2026年資本支出維持 600~700億元(約與 2025年 640億元相當)，其中 90%用於新加坡廠、10%
用於 8吋年度設備維修及優化，新加坡 VSMC 12 吋廠維持 1Q27量產的規劃，預期 2028年底即
可達到每月 4.4 萬片產能，並維持 90%以上產能利用率，其中 50%以上營收將來自台積電外包的
Interposer，1Q27量產後將導致 2027年折舊顯著增加超過 40%，但整體產品組合佳，且技術及設
備來自台積電、大幅縮短學習曲線，仍預期 2027年底即可達成損平，預估 2027年 12吋晶圓將占
營收 10%、2028年提升至 21%。

產業概況  
台灣晶圓代工產值年增 34%，成熟製程產業狀況改善 
台灣半導體產業持續受惠 AI需求發展，先進製程優先受惠，預估 2026年台灣晶圓代工產業產值年
增 34%，而台積電的市占率約 94%，營收成長仍優於其他晶圓代工同業，而台積電規劃將 6吋、8
吋成熟製程產能轉作利潤更高的先進封裝，預估 2027年每月將有約 9萬片的產能釋出，假設轉單
至台系成熟製程廠商，約可提升聯電、世界先進的成熟製程產能利用率 10~15%，其中世界的新加
坡 12吋廠預計 2027年投產，較有望承接來自台積電的轉單效益，預估可增加 1~1.5元 EPS，受
惠程度較大。而整體成熟製程仍受惠 AI需求爆發所帶來的外溢效果，產業狀況持續改善。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 11月 9日 Vanguard 第5 頁，共 14頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
仍看好 2027 年 12 吋廠及 AI 應用貢獻，維持買進 
2027 年 PMIC 將維持供不應求、成熟製程需求將出現結構性改變，受 AI 需求強勁排擠產能，
PC/NB/手機等消費性應用雖然需求不佳，但成熟製程產能利用率仍為 90%以上，世界的 PMIC 比
重已達 82%，預估 2026年 AI相關比重預估將由 2025年的 0~5%倍增至 10~15%，加上新加坡 12
吋廠 VSMC預計在 1Q27起量產台積電 CoWoS的 Interposer，且良率表現優於預期，2027年 AI
比重有望挑戰 30%。 
將 2026/2027 年營收預估分別上修 1.3%/1.3%至 591.1 億元/798.7 億元，2027 年起折舊將因
VSMC量產而大幅提高，將 2026/2027年稅後 EPS預估調整至 6.32元/8.57元、分別上修 0.7%/
下修 11.1%。短期股價修正，但以 2027年獲利計算目前 PE評價僅約 14倍，低於過去 10年平均
約 20倍，且仍看好 2027年起跨入 12吋業務、AI比重提升，將有利於評價回升，維持買進建議，
目標價 170元(20X 2027F EPS)反應獲利預估因折舊而下調。

公司簡介  
世界先進設立於 1994年，主要業務為晶圓製造服務。其製程以成熟製程為主，分布於 0.5/0.35/0.25/<=0.18um等製程。主
要廠區分布於台灣以及新加坡，且多數為 8吋晶圓廠，與 NXP合資之 12吋晶圓廠 VSMC將於 2027年量產。其代工產品
主要為 LCD驅動 IC、電源管理 IC及指紋辨識 IC。客戶包含台積電、聯詠、奇景等廠商。國際間競爭廠商包含聯電、力積
電、Dongbu HiTek、GlobalFoundries、Magnachip、Tower Jazz、SMIC與華虹半導體。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 11月 9日 Vanguard 第11 頁，共 14頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 世界先進整體的 ESG風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略微
領先的位置，但在半導體設計和製造行業的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 世界先進的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與半導體設計和製造行業的平均水準相當。公司較需要改
善的 ESG 議題包含人力資本、資源利用、商業道德等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：世界先進在針對重大 ESG議題的管理水準和執行力屬高等。公司已指定董事會級別
人員負責監督 ESG 問題。此外，公司實行強大的環境政策。然該公司的高管薪酬並未明確與 ESG 績效目標掛鉤，不過
吹哨者計畫較完善。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

## Document 592: 凱基投顧_3026 禾伸堂_鄭宇宏_20261110

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_3026 禾伸堂_鄭宇宏_20261110.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  被動元件 ‧ 台灣

禾伸堂 
(3026. TW /3026 TT)

NP0 及 Class 2 MLCC 雙引擎，成為中長期主要動能  
重要訊息 
公司召開 3Q26 法說會。  
評論及分析  
產業進入門檻高，核心供應地位穩固。 管理層表示 3Q26 在 AI 營收比重達
35%，並目標 4Q26 達 38%。據了解，目前 333 1k V NP0 尚未成為主要出
貨規格，我們預期隨下一代伺服器平台出貨逐漸放量，加上其開始導入三相
電源設計，規格及用量將大幅提升；雖日系廠商亦有相同技術，但其更 聚焦
在其他領域。另一方面，由於 PSU 內被動元件需滿足低溫升、低耗損及高可
靠度，客戶對於新進供應商採用更為保守，加上伺服器規格升級趨勢持續加
速，大幅提升進入門檻 。據此，我們預期禾伸堂 2026-27 年市占率將維持至
少 65%、50%的主導地位。  
Class 2 MLCC 成為第二成長動能 。管理層表示未來在板端 100V 10 uF X7R 規
格之輸出電容部分將迎來快速成長，主因現行架構下面積有限，故須改採
MLCC 分壓以降低體積；禾伸堂過往即深耕相關電壓領域，且因測試環節繁
複，目前仍以日廠及禾伸堂為主要供應商，但考量單位時間及產值，日廠更
聚焦市場規模更大之小尺寸高容值產品，我們認為該類 MLCC 將成為除 NP0
外禾伸堂另一成長動能。  
預估 2025-27 年 EPS CAGR 達 141%。管理層表示 2026-27 年產能將分別
增加 30%、40%，設備將於 4Q26 起陸續進駐，同時透過良率提升及產能調
整盡力滿足客戶需求，雖 近期電源廠恐受缺料、 HVDC 遞延影響，但在高度
供不應求下我們認為 對公司影響有限。我們保守下修 2027 年出貨量假設，
故小幅下調 2027 年營收 3%至 256 億元，其中 AI 相關 MLCC 比重將達到
64%，惟因公司持續進行產能調整並聚焦高階產品，毛利率預估將年增
13.1ppts 至 41.1%，帶動 EPS 年增 141%至 38.51 元。 
投資建議  
即便我們下修 2027 年獲利展望，但目前股價僅交易於 2027 年 EPS 預估之
13倍本益比，我們認為在公司產業地位穩固且具備高度成長趨勢下評價仍偏
低。我們維持禾伸堂「增加持股」評等，但小幅下修 12 個月目標價至 960
元，係基於 2027 年 EPS 預估的 25 倍。 
投資風險  
伺服器規格下降且出貨不如預期；日系同業積極擴產搶佔市佔率。  
增加持股 ‧維持 
  收盤價 November 10 (NT$)   510

前次目標價  (NT$) 1,000

焦點內容

年在 AI 伺服器 PSU NP0 MLCC 仍可維
持 65%、50%的市占率。 
2. 功率提升但板端面積有限，預期大尺寸
耐高壓且高容值之 Class 2 MLCC 將成
為第二成長動能。 
3. 供不應求持續， NP0 及 Class 2 MLCC 同
步成長，預估 2025 -27 年 EPS CAGR 達
141%。
